Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). ```text
1	\section{Experiments}
2	\label{sec:experiments}
3	
4	We analyze 11 of ScienceWorld's 30 task types (245 episodes); the 19 excluded types (63\%) lack actionable routing signal due to development contamination (5), taxonomic redundancy (7), or agent-floor performance (7). Taxonomically redundant tasks share identical action vocabularies, reward functions, and environmental dynamics, differing only in target entity; we retain one representative per family (Appendix~\ref{app:per-task}). This scope limits generalizability: conclusions apply to the included subset; increasing the 30-step budget might surface signal in excluded types (see Limitations). An anti-cherry-picking analysis confirms that death dominance holds in 18 of these 19 types, with a mean $\Delta\text{DR} = +38.2$\,pp exceeding the included tasks' $+28.7$\,pp (Appendix~\ref{app:per-task}). Extended validation at $n{=}20$ on five representative excluded tasks confirms this direction ($\overline{\Delta\text{DR}} = +32.0$\,pp; Appendix~\ref{app:extended-validation}). A reasoning-depth ablation ($n{=}120$) found no significant difference between internal strategy variants ($p > 0.26$; Appendix~\ref{app:ablations}). Prescreening used $n{=}5$ episodes per task type; directional consistency (18/19 types favoring deterministic) rather than point estimates drives the inclusion criterion.
5	
6	\subsection{Death Avoidance Decomposition}
7	\label{sec:death-decomp}
8	
9	\begin{figure}[t]
10	\centering
11	\includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figures/label_decomposition.pdf}
12	\caption{Episode-level label decomposition ($N{=}245$). Of 95 non-tie labels, 75 (78.9\%) arise from death asymmetry: 54 where death is the sole discriminator and 21 with co-occurring score differences. All 20 non-death labels originate from a single task type (\texttt{find-non-living-thing}).}
13	\label{fig:label-decomposition}
14	\end{figure}
15	
16	Of the 95 non-tie routing labels (Figure~\ref{fig:label-decomposition}), 75 (78.9\%; 95\% CI: $[70.7\%, 87.0\%]$, episode-level bootstrap) arise from death asymmetry: the internal strategy triggers a catastrophic $-100$ penalty while the deterministic strategy survives. This penalty-independent label fraction is the primary diagnostic metric. Of these, 54 are sole-signal labels where death was the only discriminator; at $P{=}0$ these reduce to ties, but the labeling function itself is penalty-independent (Algorithm~\ref{alg:labeling}).
17	
18	Of 245 episodes, 150 (61.2\%) show no preference between strategies; routing is relevant in the remaining 95 (38.8\%). Among these, death avoidance dominates. The Death Avoidance Factor, a derived penalty-dependent measure, quantifies death's contribution to the total performance gap: DAF $= 90.4\%$ at $P{=}100$ ($\Delta_{\text{full}} = +29.60$, $\Delta_{\text{nd}} = +2.84$, 95\% CI$_{\text{nd}}$: $[0.34, 5.57]$; 95\% CI$_{\text{DAF}}$: $[81.3\%, 99.1\%]$, episode-level bootstrap, $B{=}10{,}000$). DAF varies monotonically with $P$ (\S\ref{sec:daf}; Appendix~\ref{app:penalty}), confirming that the 78.9\% label fraction provides the more robust characterization. When $\Delta_{\text{nd}} \to 0$, DAF $\to 1$ is algebraic necessity regardless of actual death dominance; conversely, when $\Delta_{\text{nd}} < 0$ (as in Reflexion $N{\geq}3$; \S\ref{sec:agent-improvement}), DAF exceeds 100\%, reflecting that death asymmetry more than accounts for the full gap.
19	
20	\begin{figure}[t]
21	\centering
22	\includegraphics[width=0.75\columnwidth]{figures/fig1_daf_scatter.pdf}
23	\caption{Per-task death rate differential vs.\ performance gap ($n{=}11$ task types). The near-perfect correlation ($r = 0.97$) is a structural consequence of death dominance (DAF $\to 1 \Rightarrow r \to 1$), not an independent finding. The sole outlier (\texttt{find-non-living-thing}, red diamond) has zero deaths; its $\Delta_{\text{full}} = +9.58$ arises entirely from non-death score differences.}
24	\label{fig:decomposition}
25	\end{figure}
26	
27	\paragraph{Per-task correlation structure.}
28	The internal strategy dies in 42.9\% of episodes versus 16.3\% for deterministic (2.6$\times$); a detailed failure-mode analysis reveals that all 105 internal-strategy deaths are triggered by the \texttt{focus} action (Appendix~\ref{app:failure-modes}). Per-task death rate differential correlates with $\Delta_{\text{full}}$ ($r = 0.97$, $p < 10^{-6}$; Figure~\ref{fig:decomposition}); since DAF$\to 1$ implies $r \to 1$ by construction, the near-perfect correlation is a consistency check, not an independent finding (Appendix~\ref{app:correlation}). Of 245 episodes, 86 are \texttt{det\_preferred}, 9 \texttt{internal\_preferred}, and 150 \texttt{no\_preference}.
29	
30	\paragraph{Signal concentration and binary structure.}
31	The Signal Concentration Index (SCI $= 0.2147$; Appendix~\ref{app:sci}) confirms low concentration: the top-3 task types account for only $39.8\%$ of total death-rate differential. The aggregate 78.9\% reflects a per-task binary structure: all 10 death-prone task types produce 100\% death-asymmetry labels, while \texttt{find-non-living-thing} (zero deaths, $\Delta = +9.58$ from non-death scores; case study in Appendix~\ref{app:non-death-case-study}) produces 0\%. The framework thus diagnoses a binary property (``is this environment death-dominated?'') rather than a continuous routing signal. A systematic decomposition of all 20 non-death labels (Appendix~\ref{app:non-death-case-study}) reveals they originate exclusively from this single task type, with hallucination-induced errors (50\%) dominating over object selection quality differences (35\%). Removing \texttt{find-non-living-thing} raises the label fraction to 100\% and yields DAF $\geq$100\% ($\Delta_\text{nd} \leq 0$; death avoidance fully accounts for the routing gap among remaining task types; Appendix~\ref{app:loto-daf}).
32	
33	\paragraph{Sensitivity and seed robustness.}
34	Non-clustered episode-level bootstrap ($B = 10{,}000$) yields 95\% CI $[81.3\%, 99.1\%]$ for DAF; cluster-robust CI $[73.3\%, 100.0\%]$ ($K{=}11$; Appendix~\ref{app:wild-cluster}). Leave-one-task-out analysis confirms stability (label fraction range: 75.9\%--78.9\%; Appendix~\ref{app:loto-daf}); two independent replications---cross-model (Gemma-2-9B-it, DAF${}\,{=}\,89.7\%$; \S\ref{sec:cross-model}) and same-model with a different environment seed (seed=123, $N{=}220$, DAF${}\,{=}\,89.7\%$; Appendix~\ref{app:seed-robustness})---both converge within 0.7~pp. Exact permutation ($p=0.002$) and sign tests ($p<0.001$) on $K{=}11$ task-type clusters provide distribution-free confirmation (Appendix~\ref{app:wild-cluster}; supplementary evidence in Appendix~\ref{app:per-task-ci}).
35	
36	\subsection{Multi-Model Validation (7--9B Tier)}
37	\label{sec:cross-model}
38	
39	A Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct~\citep{llama3-2024} cross-model replication across all 11 task types ($N{=}220$) confirms that death dominance replicates across model families: internal death rate $41.4\%$ vs.\ deterministic $11.4\%$ (death-rate differential $+30.0$~pp), DAF $= 88.2\%$ (95\% CI: $[80.2\%, 95.1\%]$; $\Delta_{\text{full}} = +33.17$, $\Delta_{\text{nd}} = +3.9$), with direction consistency 10/10 on death-prone tasks, 83 deterministic-preferred, 3 internal-preferred (1.4\%, all death-asymmetry reversals), and 134 no-preference labels.
40	Both models show ${\sim}42\%$ internal vulnerability ($+26.5$ vs.\ $+30.0$~pp differential; per-task details in Appendix~\ref{app:cross-model}).
41	A third model family, Gemma-2-9B-it~\citep{gemma2-2024} (sliding window attention, vs.\ full attention in Qwen/Llama), independently confirms the pattern: DAF${=}89.7\%$ (95\% CI $[79.5\%, 98.4\%]$; $N{=}220$), with all 10 death-prone task types showing positive $\Delta$DR (Appendix~\ref{app:cross-model}). Three architecturally distinct models thus converge on DAF $88$--$90\%$ (spread 2.2~pp); $10{\times}$ scaling to 72B (\S\ref{sec:agent-improvement}) confirms persistence across capability levels (DAF${=}92.4\%$).
42	
43	\subsection{Agent Improvement Baselines}
44	\label{sec:agent-improvement}
45	
46	\begin{table}[t]
47	\centering
48	\small
49	\begin{tabular}{@{}lrr@{}}
50	\toprule
51	Condition & Death\% & $N$ \\
52	\midrule
53	Single-trial baseline & 42.9 & 245 \\
54	Reflexion ($3{\times}$) & 31.4 & 220 \\
55	Qwen2.5-72B ($10{\times}$) & 30.6 & 255 \\
56	Deterministic baseline & 16.3 & 245 \\
57	\midrule
58	\multicolumn{3}{@{}l}{\textit{10 death-prone tasks only:}} \\
59	Death-prone baseline & 48.0 & 200 \\
60	Retry ($5{\times}$) & 43.5 & 200 \\
61	\bottomrule
62	\end{tabular}
63	\caption{Internal-strategy death rates under agent improvement methods. Top: all 11 tasks (72B: independently collected at both internal and deterministic). Bottom: 10 death-prone tasks (\texttt{find-non-living-thing} excluded, 0\% death at all scales). All interventions reduce death rate but none approaches deterministic-level safety.}
64	\label{tab:agent-improvement}
65	\end{table}
66	
67	We test whether agent-level improvement reduces death dominance (Table~\ref{tab:agent-improvement}). Retry ($N{=}5$ attempts without self-correction) yields marginal reduction on death-prone tasks ($43.5\%$ vs.\ $48.0\%$ baseline, $\Delta = -4.5$~pp). Reflexion~\citep{reflexion-2023}\footnote{Reflexion uses a fixed allocation of 20 episodes per task type ($20 \times 11 = 220$), vs.\ the baseline's variable 20--40 per type ($N{=}245$).} (verbal self-reflection between trials) reduces death to 31.4\% across all 11 tasks by rescuing 29.3\% of first-trial deaths, but the gap to deterministic remains 15.1~pp; on the 10 death-prone tasks, Reflexion achieves ${\sim}$35\%. Under Reflexion ($N{=}3$, matched-episode analysis), $\Delta_{\text{nd}} = -0.47$, yielding DAF $> 100\%$: the deterministic advantage is entirely attributable to death avoidance, and in non-death episodes the self-correcting agent slightly outperforms the deterministic baseline. As a preliminary probe, scaling to Qwen2.5-72B ($10{\times}$ parameters; both strategies independently collected at 72B, 255 episodes across all 11 task types) reduces death rate to $30.6\%$. The 72B deterministic strategy achieves $2.75\%$ death rate ($\Delta\text{DR} = +27.8$~pp), and $\Delta_{\text{nd}} = +2.28$, yielding DAF $= 92.4\%$ (95\% CI: $[86.1\%, 97.9\%]$; 7B: $90.4\%$), with 5 internal-preferred episodes (vs.\ 9 at 7B; $2.0\%$ vs.\ $3.7\%$). This $10{\times}$ scaling analysis (distinct from the 7--9B cross-family replication in \S\ref{sec:cross-model}) shows that while 72B reduces the internal death rate from $42.9\%$ to $30.6\%$, DAF \emph{increases} because the deterministic strategy also becomes safer ($2.75\%$ vs.\ $16.3\%$), making the proportional dominance of death avoidance more extreme.
68	
69	\paragraph{Dose-response analysis.}
70	We extend both interventions to $N{=}5$ trials on death-prone tasks ($n{=}200$; Figure~\ref{fig:dose-response}). Retry is nearly flat ($48.0\% \to 43.5\%$). Reflexion drops steeply to $34.0\%$ at $N{=}3$ ($\Delta = -13.5$~pp, $p = 0.003$) then stabilizes ($34$--$36.5\%$ for $N \geq 3$, pairwise $p > 0.5$); both the decline and plateau are well-powered (MDE ${\sim}8$~pp at 80\% power; Appendix~\ref{app:dose-response}).
71	
72	\begin{figure}[t]
73	\centering
74	\includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figures/dose_response.pdf}
75	\caption{Death rate as a function of trial count ($N$) for Retry and Reflexion on 10 death-prone tasks ($n{=}200$). Retry (blue) is nearly flat; Reflexion (red) drops steeply through $N{=}3$ then shows no statistically detectable improvement beyond $N{=}3$ (Wilson CIs for $N{=}3,4,5$ overlap substantially). Horizontal lines: 72B scaling baseline ($30.6\%$) and deterministic baseline ($16.3\%$), both computed over all 11 task types; retry and Reflexion use 10 death-prone types only. Error bands: Wilson 95\% CIs.}
76	\label{fig:dose-response}
77	\end{figure}
78	
79	\subsection{Cross-Environment Boundary Probes}
80	\label{sec:math-contrast}
81	\label{sec:cross-env}
82	
83	We examine MATH and ALFWorld as boundary probes (not cross-domain validation). The deterministic oracle differs in information privilege across environments---ground-truth simulator (ScienceWorld), code interpreter (MATH), admissible-action enumeration (ALFWorld; \S\ref{sec:problem-setup})---so cross-environment results validate the diagnostic procedure's reusability, not the transferability of specific findings.
84	
85	\paragraph{MATH.} On MATH Level~4--5~\citep{math-2021} ($N{=}795$, code interpreter), internal reasoning and code interpretation achieve near-identical accuracy ($73.5\%$ vs.\ $73.0\%$, McNemar $p = 0.81$), with 84.4\% no-preference. Without catastrophic failure, no routing signal emerges.
86	
87	\paragraph{ALFWorld.} ALFWorld~\citep{alfworld-2021} (6 task types, 135 games) provides the deterministic strategy with admissible actions, an information asymmetry rather than a computation mode switch, with no death mechanism ($\delta = 0$, DAF $= 0\%$). With Qwen2.5-7B, 35 of 37 non-tie labels (94.6\%) favor the information-advantaged strategy (Appendix~\ref{app:cross-env-details}); Llama-3.1-8B (135 games) yields 16 of 16 non-tie labels in the same direction (100\%). Llama shows fewer non-tie episodes (11.9\% vs.\ 25.9\%) but perfect directional consistency, confirming that information asymmetry, not death avoidance, drives routing signal in ALFWorld regardless of model family. The framework diagnoses this qualitatively different mechanism (DAF $= 0\%$ vs.\ $90.4\%$) without modification.
88	
89	\subsection{Baselines}
90	\label{sec:baselines}
91	
92	\begin{table}[t]
93	\centering
94	\small
95	\resizebox{\columnwidth}{!}{%
96	\begin{tabular}{@{}lccc@{}}
97	\toprule
98	Strategy & Score & 95\% CI & Death \% \\
99	\midrule
100	Always-Internal & $-39.07$ & [$-46.40$, $-32.27$] & 42.9 \\
101	Always-Deterministic & $-9.47$ & [$-14.71$, $-4.29$] & 16.3 \\
102	Random & $-22.38$ & [$-28.67$, $-15.75$] & 27.8 \\
103	Oracle & $-6.58$ & [$-11.54$, $-1.70$] & 14.3 \\
104	\midrule
105	Trained Router$^\dagger$ & $-10.70$ & [$-16.33$, $-5.27$] & 17.6 \\
106	\bottomrule
107	\end{tabular}}
108	\caption{Episode-weighted mean scores on ScienceWorld (11 task types, 20--40 episodes per type, 245 total). $\dagger$\,Single random seed (42) per episode pair; see Appendix~\ref{app:seed-robustness} for 8-seed robustness analysis. Per-task results are exploratory ($n{=}20$ episodes per task, power $\approx 50\%$ for 20\,pp effects); aggregate conclusions do not depend on individual task significance.}
109	\label{tab:baselines}
110	\end{table}
111	
112	Always-deterministic ($-9.47$) outperforms always-internal ($-39.07$) by 29.60 points. The oracle improves only to $-6.58$, with 96.3\% of decisions matching always-deterministic---an \emph{oracle degeneracy} where an always-deterministic lookup achieves 90.5\% of non-tie oracle decisions (balanced accuracy 50.0\%: perfect deterministic recall, zero internal recall). Even with perfect per-task death-risk knowledge, threshold routing cannot outperform the always-deterministic baseline (Appendix~\ref{sec:threshold-routing}).
113	
114	\subsection{Router Training: Inconclusive Results}
115	\label{sec:router-diagnostic}
116	
117	We probe whether counterfactual labels can train a step-level router, as a diagnostic of label informativeness. The oracle margin (2.89 points) caps routing upside. An implicit router (Qwen3, LoRA; Appendix~\ref{app:router-details}) trained at three scales (0.6B, 1.7B, 4B) shows flat step-level balanced accuracy ($56$--$59\%$); episode-level independent evaluation yields $47.1 \pm 2.2\%$, below the majority-vote baseline.\footnote{The near-chance performance is consistent with noisy-label learning bounds~\citep{natarajan2013learning,northcutt2021confident}: ${\sim}$88\% of step-level labels are uninformative, class imbalance is extreme (90.5\% deterministic-preferred, 3.7\% minority), and paraphrase-based text augmentation (25\%--800\%) provides no improvement (Appendix~\ref{app:seed-robustness}).} Leave-one-task-type-out analysis degrades to chance (49.2\%; Appendix~\ref{app:ablations}), as does a task-type-only classifier ($64.7\%$ in-distribution, $50.0\%$ LOTO), confirming routing signal is a per-task-type constant with no cross-type generalization. Deployed end-to-end, the best router scores $-10.70$ (95\% CI: $[-16.33, -5.27]$), not exceeding always-deterministic ($-9.47$). Results remain inconclusive under the current data regime ($N{=}245$, 3.7\% minority class).
118

Leave-one-task-out analysis confirms stability (label fraction range: 75.9\\%--78.9\\%; Appendix~\\ref{app:loto-daf}); two independent replications---cross-model (Gemma-2-9B-it, DAF${}\\,{=}\\,89.7\\%$; \\S\\ref{sec:cross-model}) and same-model with a different environment seed (seed=123, $N{=}220$, DAF${}\\,{=}\\,89.7\\%$; Appendix~\\ref{app:seed-robustness})---both converge within 0.7~pp. Exact permutation ($p=0.002$) and sign tests ($p<0.001$) on $K{=}11$ task-type clusters provide distribution-free confirmation (Appendix~\\ref{app:wild-cluster}; supplementary evidence in Appendix~\\ref{app:per-task-ci}).\n\n\\subsection{Multi-Model Validation (7--9B Tier)}\n\\label{sec:cross-model}\n\nA Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct~\\citep{llama3-2024} cross-model replication across all 11 task types ($N{=}220$) confirms that death dominance replicates across model families: internal death rate $41.4\\%$ vs.\\ deterministic $11.4\\%$ (death-rate differential $+30.0$~pp), DAF $= 88.2\\%$ (95\\% CI: $[80.2\\%, 95.1\\%]$; $\\Delta_{\\text{full}} = +33.17$, $\\Delta_{\\text{nd}} = +3.9$), with direction consistency 10/10 on death-prone tasks, 83 deterministic-preferred, 3 internal-preferred (1.4\\%, all death-asymmetry reversals), and 134 no-preference labels.\nBoth models show ${\\sim}42\\%$ internal vulnerability ($+26.5$ vs.\\ $+30.0$~pp differential; per-task details in Appendix~\\ref{app:cross-model}).\nA third model family, Gemma-2-9B-it~\\citep{gemma2-2024} (sliding window attention, vs.\\ full attention in Qwen/Llama), independently confirms the pattern: DAF${=}89.7\\%$ (95\\% CI $[79.5\\%, 98.4\\%]$; $N{=}220$), with all 10 death-prone task types showing positive $\\Delta$DR (Appendix~\\ref{app:cross-model}). Three architecturally distinct models thus converge on DAF $88$--$90\\%$ (spread 2.2~pp); $10{\\times}$ scaling to 72B (\\S\\ref{sec:agent-improvement}) confirms persistence across capability levels (DAF${=}92.4\\%$).\n\n\\subsection{Agent Improvement Baselines}\n\\label{sec:agent-improvement}\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\small\n\\begin{tabular}{@{}lrr@{}}\n\\toprule\nCondition & Death\\% & $N$ \\\\\n\\midrule\nSingle-trial baseline & 42.9 & 245 \\\\\nReflexion ($3{\\times}$) & 31.4 & 220 \\\\\nQwen2.5-72B ($10{\\times}$) & 30.6 & 255 \\\\\nDeterministic baseline & 16.3 & 245 \\\\\n\\midrule\n\\multicolumn{3}{@{}l}{\\textit{10 death-prone tasks only:}} \\\\\nDeath-prone baseline & 48.0 & 200 \\\\\nRetry ($5{\\times}$) & 43.5 & 200 \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}\n\\caption{Internal-strategy death rates under agent improvement methods. Top: all 11 tasks (72B: independently collected at both internal and deterministic). Bottom: 10 death-prone tasks (\\texttt{find-non-living-thing} excluded, 0\\% death at all scales). All interventions reduce death rate but none approaches deterministic-level safety.}\n\\label{tab:agent-improvement}\n\\end{table}\n\nWe test whether agent-level improvement reduces death dominance (Table~\\ref{tab:agent-improvement}). Retry ($N{=}5$ attempts without self-correction) yields marginal reduction on death-prone tasks ($43.5\\%$ vs.\\ $48.0\\%$ baseline, $\\Delta = -4.5$~pp). Reflexion~\\citep{reflexion-2023}\\footnote{Reflexion uses a fixed allocation of 20 episodes per task type ($20 \\times 11 = 220$), vs.\\ the baseline's variable 20--40 per type ($N{=}245$).} (verbal self-reflection between trials) reduces death to 31.4\\% across all 11 tasks by rescuing 29.3\\% of first-trial deaths, but the gap to deterministic remains 15.1~pp; on the 10 death-prone tasks, Reflexion achieves ${\\sim}$35\\%. Under Reflexion ($N{=}3$, matched-episode analysis), $\\Delta_{\\text{nd}} = -0.47$, yielding DAF $> 100\\%$: the deterministic advantage is entirely attributable to death avoidance, and in non-death episodes the self-correcting agent slightly outperforms the deterministic baseline. As a preliminary probe, scaling to Qwen2.5-72B ($10{\\times}$ parameters; both strategies independently collected at 72B, 255 episodes across all 11 task types) reduces death rate to $30.6\\%$. The 72B deterministic strategy achieves $2.75\\%$ death rate ($\\Delta\\text{DR} = +27.8$~pp), and $\\Delta_{\\text{nd}} = +2.28$, yielding DAF $= 92.4\\%$ (95\\% CI: $[86.1\\%, 97.9\\%]$; 7B: $90.4\\%$), with 5 internal-preferred episodes (vs.\\ 9 at 7B; $2.0\\%$ vs.\\ $3.7\\%$). This $10{\\times}$ scaling analysis (distinct from the 7--9B cross-family replication in \\S\\ref{sec:cross-model}) shows that while 72B reduces the internal death rate from $42.9\\%$ to $30.6\\%$, DAF \\emph{increases} because the deterministic strategy also becomes safer ($2.75\\%$ vs.\\ $16.3\\%$), making the proportional dominance of death avoidance more extreme.\n\n\\paragraph{Dose-response analysis.}\nWe extend both interventions to $N{=}5$ trials on death-prone tasks ($n{=}200$; Figure~\\ref{fig:dose-response}). Retry is nearly flat ($48.0\\% \\to 43.5\\%$). Reflexion drops steeply to $34.0\\%$ at $N{=}3$ ($\\Delta = -13.5$~pp, $p = 0.003$) then stabilizes ($34$--$36.5\\%$ for $N \\geq 3$, pairwise $p > 0.5$); both the decline and plateau are well-powered (MDE ${\\sim}8$~pp at 80\\% power; Appendix~\\ref{app:dose-response}).\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\columnwidth]{figures/dose_response.pdf}\n\\caption{Death rate as a function of trial count ($N$) for Retry and Reflexion on 10 death-prone tasks ($n{=}200$). Retry (blue) is nearly flat; Reflexion (red) drops steeply through $N{=}3$ then shows no statistically detectable improvement beyond $N{=}3$ (Wilson CIs for $N{=}3,4,5$ overlap substantially). Horizontal lines: 72B scaling baseline ($30.6\\%$) and deterministic baseline ($16.3\\%$), both computed over all 11 task types; retry and Reflexion use 10 death-prone types only. Error bands: Wilson 95\\% CIs.}\n\\label{fig:dose-response}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\\subsection{Cross-Environment Boundary Probes}\n\\label{sec:math-contrast}\n\\label{sec:cross-env}\n\nWe examine MATH and ALFWorld as boundary probes (not cross-domain validation). The deterministic oracle differs in information privilege across environments---ground-truth simulator (ScienceWorld), code interpreter (MATH), admissible-action enumeration (ALFWorld; \\S\\ref{sec:problem-setup})---so cross-environment results validate the diagnostic procedure's reusability, not the transferability of specific findings.\n\n\\paragraph{MATH.} On MATH Level~4--5~\\citep{math-2021} ($N{=}795$, code interpreter), internal reasoning and code interpretation achieve near-identical accuracy ($73.5\\%$ vs.\\ $73.0\\%$, McNemar $p = 0.81$), with 84.4\\% no-preference. Without catastrophic failure, no routing signal emerges.\n\n\\paragraph{ALFWorld.} ALFWorld~\\citep{alfworld-2021} (6 task types, 135 games) provides the deterministic strategy with admissible actions, an information asymmetry rather than a computation mode switch, with no death mechanism ($\\delta = 0$, DAF $= 0\\%$). With Qwen2.5-7B, 35 of 37 non-tie labels (94.6\\%) favor the information-advantaged strategy (Appendix~\\ref{app:cross-env-details}); Llama-3.1-8B (135 games) yields 16 of 16 non-tie labels in the same direction (100\\%). Llama shows fewer non-tie episodes (11.9\\% vs.\\ 25.9\\%) but perfect directional consistency, confirming that information asymmetry, not death avoidance, drives routing signal in ALFWorld regardless of model family. The framework diagnoses this qualitatively different mechanism (DAF $= 0\\%$ vs.\\ $90.4\\%$) without modification.\n\n\\subsection{Baselines}\n\\label{sec:baselines}\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\small\n\\resizebox{\\columnwidth}{!}{%\n\\begin{tabular}{@{}lccc@{}}\n\\toprule\nStrategy & Score & 95\\% CI & Death \\% \\\\\n\\midrule\nAlways-Internal & $-39.07$ & [$-46.40$, $-32.27$] & 42.9 \\\\\nAlways-Deterministic & $-9.47$ & [$-14.71$, $-4.29$] & 16.3 \\\\\nRandom & $-22.38$ & [$-28.67$, $-15.75$] & 27.8 \\\\\nOracle & $-6.58$ & [$-11.54$, $-1.70$] & 14.3 \\\\\n\\midrule\nTrained Router$^\\dagger$ & $-10.70$ & [$-16.33$, $-5.27$] & 17.6 \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}}\n\\caption{Episode-weighted mean scores on ScienceWorld (11 task types, 20--40 episodes per type, 245 total). $\\dagger$\\,Single random seed (42) per episode pair; see Appendix~\\ref{app:seed-robustness} for 8-seed robustness analysis. Per-task results are exploratory ($n{=}20$ episodes per task, power $\\approx 50\\%$ for 20\\,pp effects); aggregate conclusions do not depend on individual task significance.}\n\\label{tab:baselines}\n\\end{table}\n\nAlways-deterministic ($-9.47$) outperforms always-internal ($-39.07$) by 29.60 points. The oracle improves only to $-6.58$, with 96.3\\% of decisions matching always-deterministic---an \\emph{oracle degeneracy} where an always-deterministic lookup achieves 90.5\\% of non-tie oracle decisions (balanced accuracy 50.0\\%: perfect deterministic recall, zero internal recall). Even with perfect per-task death-risk knowledge, threshold routing cannot outperform the always-deterministic baseline (Appendix~\\ref{sec:threshold-routing}).\n\n\\subsection{Router Training: Inconclusive Results}\n\\label{sec:router-diagnostic}\n\nWe probe whether counterfactual labels can train a step-level router, as a diagnostic of label informativeness. The oracle margin (2.89 points) caps routing upside. An implicit router (Qwen3, LoRA; Appendix~\\ref{app:router-details}) trained at three scales (0.6B, 1.7B, 4B) shows flat step-level balanced accuracy ($56$--$59\\%$); episode-level independent evaluation yields $47.1 \\pm 2.2\\%$, below the majority-vote baseline.\\footnote{The near-chance performance is consistent with noisy-label learning bounds~\\citep{natarajan2013learning,northcutt2021confident}: ${\\sim}$88\\% of step-level labels are uninformative, class imbalance is extreme (90.5\\% deterministic-preferred, 3.7\\% minority), and paraphrase-based text augmentation (25\\%--800\\%) provides no improvement (Appendix~\\ref{app:seed-robustness}).} Leave-one-task-type-out analysis degrades to chance (49.2\\%; Appendix~\\ref{app:ablations}), as does a task-type-only classifier ($64.7\\%$ in-distribution, $50.0\\%$ LOTO), confirming routing signal is a per-task-type constant with no cross-type generalization. Deployed end-to-end, the best router scores $-10.70$ (95\\% CI: $[-16.33, -5.27]$), not exceeding always-deterministic ($-9.47$). Results remain inconclusive under the current data regime ($N{=}245$, 3.7\\% minority class).\n",

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01BF6vWD7uiRjDkLiCVYyFCZ`</summary>

````text
1	---
2	description: 学术论文图表设计与生成——四层指南（防丑原则 + 叙事原则 + Pattern 库 + 工具路由）。Paper Agent 规划 visualization_plan 时读，Worker 执行时也读。
3	name: paper-visualization
4	scope: global
5	tags:
6	- research
7	- visualization
8	- paper
9	- matplotlib
10	- paperbanana
11	version: 5
12	roles:
13	- agent

15	---
16	
17	# Paper Visualization — 学术图表设计与生成
18	
19	> **核心理念**：好图不是"画得对"，是"回答一个问题"。每张图动笔前先问：**这张图要让读者看到什么？**
20	>
21	> **质量底线**：每张图必须达到 NeurIPS/ICML/EMNLP best paper 的视觉水准——高级感、专业感、精致感。绝不接受 matplotlib 默认样式的廉价感。
22	
23	本 skill 分五层：
24	
25	- **L0 学习同行** — 设计前看几张优秀同类型论文图，比读任何原则都有效
26	- **L1 防丑原则** — 底线，所有图必须遵守
27	- **L2 叙事原则** — 让图有说服力（区分"数据图"和"论证图"）
28	- **L3 Pattern 库** — 具体技法目录，按 `design_notes` 选用
29	- **L4 工具路由 + 模板** — paperbanana vs matplotlib，常用统计图模板
30	
31	---
32	
33	## L0 学习同行（设计前的功课）
34	
35	你的子领域里，**最被引用、最被记住的论文是怎么画图的？** 在动笔之前花点时间去看一眼——这个动作的回报远高于读完下面的 L1-L4。
36	
37	### 为什么这一步是重要的
38	
39	- 同领域读者已经被这些论文的视觉语言"训练"过——遵循惯例 = 降低读者认知负担
40	- 顶会论文经历过 reviewer 严苛审查，能登出来的图基本说明它的 narrative 设计是被接受的
41	- 抽象原则告诉你"用 fill_between 表示发散"，但好论文告诉你"在这种数据上，发散到底应该画多淡才合适"
42	- 一个领域往往有它特有的图表惯例（如校准研究的 reliability diagram、scaling 研究的 log-log curve、RL 的 learning curve with seed bands），不看就不会用
43	
44	### 什么时候值得投入
45	
46	判断权交给你。一些可参考的信号：
47	
48	- **值得多花时间**：会决定论文成败的图（hero figure、main result、新颖的实验类型）
49	- **不太值得**：常规 supplementary 图、loss curve 这类标准化已经很高的图
50	- **强烈建议看**：你打算画一种自己不熟悉的图，或者数据形态是这个领域少见的
51	
52	### 怎么看
53	
54	工具：`paper-image-extractor` skill 可以从 arXiv source 或 PDF 提取参考论文里的 figure。你已经有 `literature_map.yaml` 里的论文清单作为起点。
55	
56	看的时候问自己（不是机械检查清单，是真的去理解）：
57	
58	- 这张图回答了什么问题？读者一眼能 get 到吗？
59	- 它用了哪些叙事层（background band / 参考线 / 概念曲线 / fill 区域）？这些层在我的数据上有对应物吗？
60	- 它的颜色和 marker 怎么承载语义？我的图能不能借鉴这套映射？
61	- 它有什么是这篇论文特有的（与他们的具体数据 / claim 绑死）？这些不要照搬。
62	- 如果让我重画它的图，我会怎么改？为什么作者没这么做？
63	
64	### 怎么把学到的传下去
65	
66	把你看完后的判断**直接写进对应 fig 的 `design_notes`**，用人话不用模板。例子：
67	
68	```yaml
69	design_notes:
70	  - "学 GPTQ Fig 4：用 log-scale bit-width + dashed FP16 baseline + 不同 marker shape 分模型族"
71	  - "Pattern A（regime band）划分 in-domain / OOD 两段"
72	  - "AWQ Fig 2 那种 hatching 区分 outlier 不适用我们的数据，跳过"
73	```
74	
75	Worker 拿到这种 design_notes 就知道既要参考 GPTQ 的 layout 又要用 Pattern A，能直接落实成代码。
76	
77	### 边界
78	
79	- 不要复刻，要消化。直接抄一张图既没用（数据不一样）也有风险（与他们的 claim 强绑定）
80	- 不要看太多。3 张就够，多了 Paper Agent 会陷入"该听谁的"
81	- 看不到原图（提取失败 / arXiv 无 source）就跳过，不要在这里卡住流程
82	
83	---
84	
85	## L1 防丑原则（底线，不可违反）
86	
87	来自 Tufte 和 Luís Cruz 16 条公认规范。Agent 默认遵守，无需逐条思考。
88	
89	1. **Self-explanatory** — 不看正文就能看懂：title/caption + 所有轴有 label + 单位
90	2. **去 chartjunk** — 不要 3D、不要背景框、不要无意义网格、不要 dual y-axis、不要 pie/donut chart
91	3. **直接标注 > Legend** — 能直接标在曲线/柱旁就不用 legend；legend 必要时不遮数据
92	4. **颜色/形状/纹理只用于 data variation** — 不用颜色装饰，不用相同颜色表示不同数据
93	5. **Meaningful baseline** — bar chart 必须从 0 起；scatter 可以非 0
94	6. **文字水平** — label 不要旋转/垂直；类别名太长 → 用 horizontal bar chart
95	7. **Sort for comparison** — 柱状图按数值排序，便于对比（除非有自然顺序如时间）
96	8. **字号一致** — 用 mm 计算 figsize（`ONE_MM = 1/25.4`），不在 LaTeX 里 `\includegraphics[scale=...]` resize
97	9. **色盲友好** — 默认 Okabe-Ito；红/绿区分时变 saturation；考虑 grayscale 打印
98	10. **Bold 只用于强调** — 不要全图加粗；highlight 留给 takeaway
99	11. **Line plot 只用于有序数据** — 类别变量用 bar
100	12. **单 x 单 y 轴** — 必须双轴时改成 multi-panel
101	13. **300 DPI、PDF 矢量、savefig.bbox=tight、去掉 top/right spines**
102	
103	---
104	
105	## L2 叙事原则（让图变成论证）
106	
107	L1 让图"不丑"，L2 让图"有说服力"。这一层是 Agent 自主画图时最容易缺失的。
108	
109	### 原则 1：每张图回答一个明确问题
110	
111	动笔前用一句话写下："This figure shows that ___."
112	
113	如果写不出来，就不要画。如果写出来的 takeaway 不是论文的 claim，就要重新设计。
114	
115	**反例**：`"Accuracy across methods"` — 这只是个标题，不是问题
116	**正例**：`"FP16 baseline 在小模型上比量化版高 5pp，但 70B 模型上差距消失"` — 这是一张图要说服读者相信的事
117	
118	`visualization_plan.{fig_id}.narrative_intent` 字段写这一句。
119	
120	### 原则 2：颜色绑定语义而非数据顺序
121	
122	错的做法：`colors = ['blue', 'orange', 'green', 'red']` 按数据顺序分配。
123	对的做法：`BN → 蓝色（始终）`、`non-BN → 橙色（始终）`、`collapse/danger → vermillion (#D55E00)`、`safe/baseline → blue (#0072B2)`。
124	
125	整篇论文 6 张图，BN 永远蓝、collapse 永远红——零认知负担，读者一眼建立映射。
126	
127	### 原则 3：信息层级用 visual variable 表达
128	
129	数据的"质量"和"分类"应该可见，不应该藏在 caption 里：
130	
131	| 信息 | Visual 表达 |
132	|------|------------|
133	| n < 50 低置信度点 | hollow marker + 标注 `n=` |
134	| Out-of-sample / pending 数据 | hollow + 不同 marker shape（如 diamond）+ "OOS" label |
135	| Collapse / 异常类别 | hatching（斜线填充）|
136	| 主结果 vs 次要结果 | 主用饱和色，次要用 muted 色或 alpha |
137	
138	### 原则 4：加叙事层，不只画数据
139	
140	数据是图的基础，叙事层是让读者"读懂"的关键。常见叙事层：
141	
142	- **Background bands** — `axhspan/axvspan` 标定性区域（"collapse zone"、"safe zone"、"Regime A/B/C"）
143	- **Fill_between** — 显示两条曲线发散区域（alpha 极低如 0.06，不抢眼但有面积）
144	- **Reference lines** — 锚定真实值（如 dashed line 标"actual W3A16 accuracy"）
145	- **Equivalence band** — `axvspan` gradient fade 标 +/-1pp 等价区间
146	- **Concept curve** — 完全合成的曲线（如 cosine trajectory）建立理论直觉
147	
148	这些都不来自数据，但帮读者"看到"数据想说什么。
149	
150	### 原则 5：Subtitle / panel label 写 insight 不写描述
151	
152	错：`"Layer 6 comparison across three models"`
153	对：`"Same relative position, opposite function"`
154	
155	错：`"Reliability diagram"`
156	对：`"Quantization shifts confidence but not accuracy"`
157	
158	副标题是免费的 takeaway，不要浪费。
159	
160	### 原则 6：Multi-panel 每个面板独立回答一个 sub-question
161	
162	不要做"主图 + 装饰小图"。每个 panel 必须有独立的信息价值。
163	
164	如果一个 panel 想不出独立的问题，要么删掉，要么合并到主 panel。
165	
166	布局上：非对称比例（如 1.4:1）让视觉重心对齐叙事重心。
167	
168	### 原则 7：所有图至少 3 轮迭代，Hero figure 至少 5 轮
169	
170	第一版画出来基本不能直接用。计划好预算：先确定数据和布局，再调样式标注，最后 polish。不要指望一次到位。
171	
172	### Hero Figure 定义
173	
174	Hero Figure = Figure 1 + 主结果图（main result figure）。这两类图对论文第一印象影响最大，必须投入更多迭代。
175	
176	---
177	
178	## L3 Pattern 库（按 `design_notes` 选用）
179	
180	按"图要回答的问题"选 pattern。每个 pattern 给出**何时用**、**怎么用**（具体代码或 matplotlib API）。
181	
182	### Pattern A：定性区域 / regime bands
183	
184	**何时用**：x 或 y 轴有自然分段（regime、phase、severity tier）。
185	
186	```python
187	# horizontal bands
188	for lo, hi, color, label in [(0, 1, '#EBF5FB', 'Regime A'), (1, 5, '#EAFAF1', 'Regime B')]:
189	    ax.axhspan(lo, hi, alpha=0.25, color=color, zorder=0)
190	    ax.text(x_label_pos, (lo+hi)/2, label, va='center', fontsize=9, color='gray')
191	
192	# 单一 collapse zone
193	ax.axhspan(-5, 5, color='#FDEDEC', alpha=0.5, zorder=0)
194	ax.text(0.02, 2.5, 'collapse zone', fontsize=8, color='#ccaaaa', fontstyle='italic')
195	```
196	
197	### Pattern B：发散面积 fill_between
198	
199	**何时用**：两组数据从某点开始发散，想让"发散程度"可见。
200	
201	```python
202	# alpha 0.06 极淡，不抢眼但有面积
203	ax.fill_between(x_range, curve_high, curve_low, alpha=0.06, color='#555555', zorder=1)
204	```
205	
206	### Pattern C：参考线锚定真实值
207	
208	**何时用**：合成 / 缩放后的 x 轴需要锚定到真实物理量。
209	
210	```python
211	ax.axhline(y=actual_value, color=color, linestyle=':', linewidth=0.8, alpha=0.3)
212	t = ax.text(x_pos, actual_value+1, 'actual W3A16', fontsize=7, color=color, alpha=0.5)
213	t.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])  # 防遮挡
214	```
215	
216	### Pattern D：信息层级 marker（hollow vs solid）
217	
218	**何时用**：同一图中有不同置信度 / OOS / pending 数据。
219	
220	```python
221	# 实心 = n >= 50, 空心 = n < 50
222	if n < 50:
223	    ax.plot(x, y, marker='o', markerfacecolor='white', markeredgecolor=color,
224	            markeredgewidth=1.2, markersize=6)
225	    ax.annotate(f'n={n}', (x, y), xytext=(0, 7), textcoords='offset points',
226	                fontsize=5, color=color, fontstyle='italic')
227	```
228	
229	### Pattern E：Wilson CI / bootstrap 不确定性
230	
231	**何时用**：accuracy / proportion 数据需要置信区间。
232	
233	```python
234	def wilson_ci(p, n, z=1.96):
235	    denom = 1 + z**2 / n
236	    center = (p + z**2 / (2*n)) / denom
237	    half = z * np.sqrt((p*(1-p) + z**2/(4*n)) / n) / denom
238	    return max(0, center - half), min(1, center + half)
239	```
240	
241	### Pattern F：Per-label offset dict（解决标注重叠）
242	
243	**何时用**：scatter 上多个点需要标注且自动布局重叠。
244	
245	```python
246	label_offsets = {
247	    'Qwen3-8B':  (6, -12),   # 向下避让
248	    'Qwen3-4B':  (-55, 4),   # 向左避让
249	    'Llama-8B':  (6, 6),
250	}
251	for name, (x, y) in data.items():
252	    ox, oy = label_offsets.get(name, (6, 6))
253	    ax.annotate(name, (x, y), xytext=(ox, oy), textcoords='offset points', fontsize=7)
254	```
255	
256	### Pattern G：Hatching 加分类维度
257	
258	**何时用**：已用颜色表示一个维度，再加一个二元维度（如 collapse vs not）。
259	
260	```python
261	for is_collapse, bar in zip(collapse_flags, bars):
262	    if is_collapse:
263	        bar.set_hatch('///')
264	        bar.set_alpha(0.7)
265	```
266	
267	### Pattern H：Multi-panel 非对称布局
268	
269	**何时用**：主图 + 辅助 panel；或两个等重要 panel 但视觉重心需要倾斜。
270	
271	```python
272	fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(6.5, 3.2),
273	                          gridspec_kw={'width_ratios': [1.4, 1]})
274	# 主面板 1.4x 宽，副面板 1x；视觉重心在左
275	```
276	
277	### Pattern I：Broken axis（异常值或对数 0）
278	
279	**何时用**：数据范围跨度大，或对数轴需要表示 0。
280	
281	```python
282	d = 0.012
283	kwargs = dict(transform=ax.transAxes, color='black', clip_on=False, linewidth=0.6)
284	ax.plot((1-d, 1+d), (-d, +d), **kwargs)  # 右下角斜线
285	ax.plot((1-d, 1+d), (1-d, 1+d), **kwargs)  # 右上角斜线
286	```
287	
288	### Pattern J：Path-effect 文字描边
289	
290	**何时用**：标注必须放在数据点 / colored region 上方。
291	
292	```python
293	import matplotlib.patheffects as pe
294	t = ax.text(x, y, 'label', fontsize=8, color='red')
295	t.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])
296	```
297	
298	### Pattern K：Concept curve（建立直觉）
299	
300	**何时用**：论文核心 claim 是定性现象，需要先让读者理解理论预期。
301	
302	```python
303	# 完全合成的 cosine trajectory，不是实验数据
304	x = np.linspace(0, 1, 100)
305	trajectory = baseline * (1 - np.cos(np.pi * x)) / 2
306	ax.plot(x, trajectory, linewidth=2, solid_capstyle='round')
307	ax.annotate('gap eliminated', (1.0, final_y), color='#388E3C',
308	            fontstyle='italic', fontsize=9)
309	```
310	
311	### Pattern L：Forest plot（meta-analysis 风格）
312	
313	**何时用**：多个子条件的 effect size + CI 对比，加 inline 统计数字。
314	
315	3-column GridSpec：(1) 标签 (2) CI 区间图 (3) 统计数字（mean, CI, p-value）
316	
317	每行：dot + horizontal line(CI) + 数字 inline。等价带用半透明 axvspan。
318	
319	### 概念图 L1 防丑原则（7 条）
320	
321	概念图（架构图、流程图、framework 示意）的视觉底线，与统计图 L1 互补。
322	
323	1. **Flow direction 一致** — 全图统一左→右或上→下，不混合方向
324	2. **Box 规格统一** — 同层级 box 尺寸、圆角、边框粗细一致
325	3. **箭头样式统一** — 不混用多种箭头类型（除非有语义区分）
326	4. **层级清晰** — 不同抽象层用缩进/嵌套/背景色区分
327	5. **密度控制** — 单张图 ≤15 个 box，超过则拆分或分组
328	6. **Label 完整** — 每个 box 和箭头都有文字说明
329	7. **无装饰性元素** — 不加纯装饰图标、渐变、3D 效果
330	
331	### 概念图 L2 叙事原则（6 条）
332	
333	让概念图不只是"画了个框图"，而是"引导读者理解系统"。
334	
335	1. **视觉锚点** — 核心贡献用饱和色/大字/加粗边框突出，其余 muted
336	2. **阅读路径** — 沿箭头方向自然读完全图，不需要跳跃
337	3. **对比布局** — 左右并列展示 before/after 或 baseline/ours
338	4. **分组嵌套** — 用浅色背景框将相关组件分组（如 encoder/decoder）
339	5. **箭头语义** — 实线=数据流，虚线=控制流/可选，粗线=关键路径
340	6. **信息层级** — 主路径用饱和色+大字，辅助信息用灰色+小字
341	
342	### 概念图 L3 Pattern 库
343	
344	| Pattern | 名称 | 适用场景 |
345	|---------|------|---------|
346	| P-A | 线性流程 | 数据处理 pipeline、推理流程 |
347	| P-B | 层级嵌套 | 模型架构（encoder/decoder/attention） |
348	| P-C | 对比并列 | baseline vs ours、before/after |
349	| P-D | 分支汇聚 | 多输入融合、ensemble |
350	| P-E | 循环 | 训练循环、迭代优化 |
351	| P-F | 诊断链 | 消融实验逻辑、排错流程 |
352	| P-G | 三面板综合 | 概念+数据+结论（常用于 Figure 1） |
353	
354	### 概念图 Checklist（7 项）
355	
356	- [ ] Flow direction 全图统一（左→右或上→下）
357	- [ ] Box 规格一致（同层级尺寸、圆角、边框相同）
358	- [ ] 视觉层级清晰（核心突出，辅助 muted）
359	- [ ] 核心贡献在视觉上最突出
360	- [ ] 箭头语义一致（实线/虚线/粗细有意义）
361	- [ ] 留白充足（box 之间不拥挤）
362	- [ ] 跨图风格一致（配色、字号、线宽与论文其他图统一）
363	
364	---
365	
366	## L4 工具路由 + 模板
367	
368	### 路由规则
369	
370	| 场景 | 工具 | 原因 |
371	|------|------|------|
372	| 概念图（架构图、流程图、framework 示意） | **PaperBanana（Gemini）** | matplotlib 手写 boxes/arrows 极痛苦 |
373	| 统计图（scatter、bar、line、heatmap、forest） | **matplotlib** | 确定性、可控、有模板 |
374	| 离线 / 无 GOOGLE_API_KEY | **matplotlib** | 不依赖外部 API |
375	
376	项目级偏好：`config/projects/{name}.yaml` 的 `visualization.preferred_tool` 可覆盖。
377	
378	### Matplotlib 通用样式（rcParams 标准）
379	
380	```python
381	import matplotlib
382	matplotlib.use('Agg')
383	import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
384	import matplotlib.patheffects as pe
385	import numpy as np
386	
387	plt.rcParams.update({
388	    'font.family': 'DejaVu Sans',
389	    'font.size': 11, 'axes.titlesize': 13, 'axes.labelsize': 12,
390	    'xtick.labelsize': 10, 'ytick.labelsize': 10, 'legend.fontsize': 10,
391	    'figure.dpi': 300, 'savefig.dpi': 300,
392	    'savefig.bbox': 'tight', 'savefig.pad_inches': 0.05,
393	    'axes.spines.top': False, 'axes.spines.right': False,
394	    'pdf.fonttype': 42, 'ps.fonttype': 42,  # TrueType 嵌入
395	    'lines.linewidth': 1.8,
396	})
397	
398	# 色盲友好语义色板（Okabe-Ito）
399	SEMANTIC_COLORS = {
400	    'baseline':   '#0072B2',  # blue - safe / mild
401	    'treatment':  '#E69F00',  # orange - intervention
402	    'collapse':   '#D55E00',  # vermillion - danger / catastrophic
403	    'safe':       '#009E73',  # green - good outcome
404	    'neutral':    '#999999',  # gray - reference / control
405	}
406	```
407	
408	### 常用模板（仅作起点，需按 narrative_intent 改造）
409	
410	详细执行模板见 `skills/global/draw-paper-figure.skill.md`：
411	- Reliability Diagram（校准图）
412	- ECE Heatmap（方法 × 数据集 ΔECE）
413	- T* Scatter Plot（任务 Regime 可视化）
414	
415	**重要**：这些模板是起点不是终点。每张图根据 `narrative_intent` 必须加 L3 中的相关 pattern，不能直接套用。
416	
417	### 主图（Figure 1）特别提示
418	
419	大多数 ML 论文的 Figure 1 是概念图/架构图/方法概览——正是 PaperBanana 的强项。除非 Figure 1 是纯数值结果对比（如 Pareto frontier），否则优先用 PaperBanana。Figure 1 的 `narrative_intent` 必须与 `writing_plan.yaml` 的 `meta.core_claim` 直接关联。
420	
421	### PaperBanana 用法
422	
423	见 `skills/global/paper-banana.skill.md`。意图描述时直接引用 `narrative_intent`，并把 L3 中的 pattern 名写进 intent（如 "with collapse zone background band, hollow markers for n<50"）。
424	
425	---
426	
427	## 给 Paper Agent 的填写规则
428	
429	规划 `visualization_plan` 时，对每张图填：
430	
431	```yaml
432	- fig_id: fig2_main_result
433	  type: statistical
434	  tool: matplotlib
435	  data_source: "registry: exp_001, exp_002"
436	  placement: experiments
437	
438	  # 新字段（必填）
439	  narrative_intent: "Quantized models match FP16 on accuracy but degrade calibration; ECE gap widens with bits→2."
440	  design_notes:
441	    - "Pattern A: regime bands for bits=8/4/3"
442	    - "Pattern C: dashed line for FP16 ECE baseline"
443	    - "Pattern D: hollow markers for n<50 data points"
444	
445	  aspect_ratio: "4:3"
446	```
447	
448	`narrative_intent`：一句话，主动语态，描述读者应该相信的事。
449	
450	`design_notes`：从 L3 选 0-4 个 pattern，给出选用理由。可以为空（说明默认模板足够）。
451	
452	---
453	
454	## 提交前 Checklist（以下为统计图专用）
455	
456	L1 部分（防丑底线）：
457	- [ ] 所有轴有 label + 单位
458	- [ ] 无 dual y-axis、无 3D、无 pie
459	- [ ] 颜色绑定语义、色盲友好
460	- [ ] Bar chart 从 0 起（如有）
461	- [ ] 字号 ≥ 10pt（缩到 column width 后仍可读）
462	- [ ] PDF 矢量、300 DPI
463	
464	L2 部分（叙事强度）：
465	- [ ] 能用一句话写出 figure takeaway（应等于 `narrative_intent`）
466	- [ ] Subtitle / panel label 是 insight，不是描述
467	- [ ] 数据质量差异在视觉上可见（n、OOS、pending 等）
468	- [ ] 至少考虑过是否需要 background band / fill_between / reference line（不一定要加）
469	
470	L3 部分（高级感——不可跳过）：
471	- [ ] 不像 matplotlib gallery 示例（无默认灰背景、无粗网格、无 tab10 色板）
472	- [ ] 配色 muted 和谐，不刺眼，不过度饱和
473	- [ ] 线宽、字号、间距全图统一
474	- [ ] 元素对齐严格（bars 底部对齐、labels 水平对齐）
475	- [ ] 留白充足（元素之间不拥挤）
476	- [ ] 与论文全文其他图的风格一致
477	- [ ] 终极判断：这张图放在 NeurIPS best paper 里违和吗？
478	
````

**核心理念**：好图不是\"画得对\"，是\"回答一个问题\"。每张图动笔前先问：**这张图要让读者看到什么？**\n>\n> **质量底线**：每张图必须达到 NeurIPS/ICML/EMNLP best paper 的视觉水准——高级感、专业感、精致感。绝不接受 matplotlib 默认样式的廉价感。\n\n本 skill 分五层：\n\n- **L0 学习同行** — 设计前看几张优秀同类型论文图，比读任何原则都有效\n- **L1 防丑原则** — 底线，所有图必须遵守\n- **L2 叙事原则** — 让图有说服力（区分\"数据图\"和\"论证图\"）\n- **L3 Pattern 库** — 具体技法目录，按 `design_notes` 选用\n- **L4 工具路由 + 模板** — paperbanana vs matplotlib，常用统计图模板\n\n---\n\n## L0 学习同行（设计前的功课）\n\n你的子领域里，**最被引用、最被记住的论文是怎么画图的？** 在动笔之前花点时间去看一眼——这个动作的回报远高于读完下面的 L1-L4。\n\n### 为什么这一步是重要的\n\n- 同领域读者已经被这些论文的视觉语言\"训练\"过——遵循惯例 = 降低读者认知负担\n- 顶会论文经历过 reviewer 严苛审查，能登出来的图基本说明它的 narrative 设计是被接受的\n- 抽象原则告诉你\"用 fill_between 表示发散\"，但好论文告诉你\"在这种数据上，发散到底应该画多淡才合适\"\n- 一个领域往往有它特有的图表惯例（如校准研究的 reliability diagram、scaling 研究的 log-log curve、RL 的 learning curve with seed bands），不看就不会用\n\n### 什么时候值得投入\n\n判断权交给你。一些可参考的信号：\n\n- **值得多花时间**：会决定论文成败的图（hero figure、main result、新颖的实验类型）\n- **不太值得**：常规 supplementary 图、loss curve 这类标准化已经很高的图\n- **强烈建议看**：你打算画一种自己不熟悉的图，或者数据形态是这个领域少见的\n\n### 怎么看\n\n工具：`paper-image-extractor` skill 可以从 arXiv source 或 PDF 提取参考论文里的 figure。你已经有 `literature_map.yaml` 里的论文清单作为起点。\n\n看的时候问自己（不是机械检查清单，是真的去理解）：\n\n- 这张图回答了什么问题？读者一眼能 get 到吗？\n- 它用了哪些叙事层（background band / 参考线 / 概念曲线 / fill 区域）？这些层在我的数据上有对应物吗？\n- 它的颜色和 marker 怎么承载语义？我的图能不能借鉴这套映射？\n- 它有什么是这篇论文特有的（与他们的具体数据 / claim 绑死）？这些不要照搬。\n- 如果让我重画它的图，我会怎么改？为什么作者没这么做？\n\n### 怎么把学到的传下去\n\n把你看完后的判断**直接写进对应 fig 的 `design_notes`**，用人话不用模板。例子：\n\n```yaml\ndesign_notes:\n  - \"学 GPTQ Fig 4：用 log-scale bit-width + dashed FP16 baseline + 不同 marker shape 分模型族\"\n  - \"Pattern A（regime band）划分 in-domain / OOD 两段\"\n  - \"AWQ Fig 2 那种 hatching 区分 outlier 不适用我们的数据，跳过\"\n```\n\nWorker 拿到这种 design_notes 就知道既要参考 GPTQ 的 layout 又要用 Pattern A，能直接落实成代码。\n\n### 边界\n\n- 不要复刻，要消化。直接抄一张图既没用（数据不一样）也有风险（与他们的 claim 强绑定）\n- 不要看太多。3 张就够，多了 Paper Agent 会陷入\"该听谁的\"\n- 看不到原图（提取失败 / arXiv 无 source）就跳过，不要在这里卡住流程\n\n---\n\n## L1 防丑原则（底线，不可违反）\n\n来自 Tufte 和 Luís Cruz 16 条公认规范。Agent 默认遵守，无需逐条思考。\n\n1. **Self-explanatory** — 不看正文就能看懂：title/caption + 所有轴有 label + 单位\n2. **去 chartjunk** — 不要 3D、不要背景框、不要无意义网格、不要 dual y-axis、不要 pie/donut chart\n3. **直接标注 > Legend** — 能直接标在曲线/柱旁就不用 legend；legend 必要时不遮数据\n4. **颜色/形状/纹理只用于 data variation** — 不用颜色装饰，不用相同颜色表示不同数据\n5. **Meaningful baseline** — bar chart 必须从 0 起；scatter 可以非 0\n6. **文字水平** — label 不要旋转/垂直；类别名太长 → 用 horizontal bar chart\n7. **Sort for comparison** — 柱状图按数值排序，便于对比（除非有自然顺序如时间）\n8. **字号一致** — 用 mm 计算 figsize（`ONE_MM = 1/25.4`），不在 LaTeX 里 `\\includegraphics[scale=...]` resize\n9. **色盲友好** — 默认 Okabe-Ito；红/绿区分时变 saturation；考虑 grayscale 打印\n10. **Bold 只用于强调** — 不要全图加粗；highlight 留给 takeaway\n11. **Line plot 只用于有序数据** — 类别变量用 bar\n12. **单 x 单 y 轴** — 必须双轴时改成 multi-panel\n13. **300 DPI、PDF 矢量、savefig.bbox=tight、去掉 top/right spines**\n\n---\n\n## L2 叙事原则（让图变成论证）\n\nL1 让图\"不丑\"，L2 让图\"有说服力\"。这一层是 Agent 自主画图时最容易缺失的。\n\n### 原则 1：每张图回答一个明确问题\n\n动笔前用一句话写下：\"This figure shows that ___.\"\n\n如果写不出来，就不要画。如果写出来的 takeaway 不是论文的 claim，就要重新设计。\n\n**反例**：`\"Accuracy across methods\"` — 这只是个标题，不是问题\n**正例**：`\"FP16 baseline 在小模型上比量化版高 5pp，但 70B 模型上差距消失\"` — 这是一张图要说服读者相信的事\n\n`visualization_plan.{fig_id}.narrative_intent` 字段写这一句。\n\n### 原则 2：颜色绑定语义而非数据顺序\n\n错的做法：`colors = ['blue', 'orange', 'green', 'red']` 按数据顺序分配。\n对的做法：`BN → 蓝色（始终）`、`non-BN → 橙色（始终）`、`collapse/danger → vermillion (#D55E00)`、`safe/baseline → blue (#0072B2)`。\n\n整篇论文 6 张图，BN 永远蓝、collapse 永远红——零认知负担，读者一眼建立映射。\n\n### 原则 3：信息层级用 visual variable 表达\n\n数据的\"质量\"和\"分类\"应该可见，不应该藏在 caption 里：\n\n| 信息 | Visual 表达 |\n|------|------------|\n| n < 50 低置信度点 | hollow marker + 标注 `n=` |\n| Out-of-sample / pending 数据 | hollow + 不同 marker shape（如 diamond）+ \"OOS\" label |\n| Collapse / 异常类别 | hatching（斜线填充）|\n| 主结果 vs 次要结果 | 主用饱和色，次要用 muted 色或 alpha |\n\n### 原则 4：加叙事层，不只画数据\n\n数据是图的基础，叙事层是让读者\"读懂\"的关键。常见叙事层：\n\n- **Background bands** — `axhspan/axvspan` 标定性区域（\"collapse zone\"、\"safe zone\"、\"Regime A/B/C\"）\n- **Fill_between** — 显示两条曲线发散区域（alpha 极低如 0.06，不抢眼但有面积）\n- **Reference lines** — 锚定真实值（如 dashed line 标\"actual W3A16 accuracy\"）\n- **Equivalence band** — `axvspan` gradient fade 标 +/-1pp 等价区间\n- **Concept curve** — 完全合成的曲线（如 cosine trajectory）建立理论直觉\n\n这些都不来自数据，但帮读者\"看到\"数据想说什么。\n\n### 原则 5：Subtitle / panel label 写 insight 不写描述\n\n错：`\"Layer 6 comparison across three models\"`\n对：`\"Same relative position, opposite function\"`\n\n错：`\"Reliability diagram\"`\n对：`\"Quantization shifts confidence but not accuracy\"`\n\n副标题是免费的 takeaway，不要浪费。\n\n### 原则 6：Multi-panel 每个面板独立回答一个 sub-question\n\n不要做\"主图 + 装饰小图\"。每个 panel 必须有独立的信息价值。\n\n如果一个 panel 想不出独立的问题，要么删掉，要么合并到主 panel。\n\n布局上：非对称比例（如 1.4:1）让视觉重心对齐叙事重心。\n\n### 原则 7：所有图至少 3 轮迭代，Hero figure 至少 5 轮\n\n第一版画出来基本不能直接用。计划好预算：先确定数据和布局，再调样式标注，最后 polish。不要指望一次到位。\n\n### Hero Figure 定义\n\nHero Figure = Figure 1 + 主结果图（main result figure）。这两类图对论文第一印象影响最大，必须投入更多迭代。\n\n---\n\n## L3 Pattern 库（按 `design_notes` 选用）\n\n按\"图要回答的问题\"选 pattern。每个 pattern 给出**何时用**、**怎么用**（具体代码或 matplotlib API）。\n\n### Pattern A：定性区域 / regime bands\n\n**何时用**：x 或 y 轴有自然分段（regime、phase、severity tier）。\n\n```python\n# horizontal bands\nfor lo, hi, color, label in [(0, 1, '#EBF5FB', 'Regime A'), (1, 5, '#EAFAF1', 'Regime B')]:\n    ax.axhspan(lo, hi, alpha=0.25, color=color, zorder=0)\n    ax.text(x_label_pos, (lo+hi)/2, label, va='center', fontsize=9, color='gray')\n\n# 单一 collapse zone\nax.axhspan(-5, 5, color='#FDEDEC', alpha=0.5, zorder=0)\nax.text(0.02, 2.5, 'collapse zone', fontsize=8, color='#ccaaaa', fontstyle='italic')\n```\n\n### Pattern B：发散面积 fill_between\n\n**何时用**：两组数据从某点开始发散，想让\"发散程度\"可见。\n\n```python\n# alpha 0.06 极淡，不抢眼但有面积\nax.fill_between(x_range, curve_high, curve_low, alpha=0.06, color='#555555', zorder=1)\n```\n\n### Pattern C：参考线锚定真实值\n\n**何时用**：合成 / 缩放后的 x 轴需要锚定到真实物理量。\n\n```python\nax.axhline(y=actual_value, color=color, linestyle=':', linewidth=0.8, alpha=0.3)\nt = ax.text(x_pos, actual_value+1, 'actual W3A16', fontsize=7, color=color, alpha=0.5)\nt.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])  # 防遮挡\n```\n\n### Pattern D：信息层级 marker（hollow vs solid）\n\n**何时用**：同一图中有不同置信度 / OOS / pending 数据。\n\n```python\n# 实心 = n >= 50, 空心 = n < 50\nif n < 50:\n    ax.plot(x, y, marker='o', markerfacecolor='white', markeredgecolor=color,\n            markeredgewidth=1.2, markersize=6)\n    ax.annotate(f'n={n}', (x, y), xytext=(0, 7), textcoords='offset points',\n                fontsize=5, color=color, fontstyle='italic')\n```\n\n### Pattern E：Wilson CI / bootstrap 不确定性\n\n**何时用**：accuracy / proportion 数据需要置信区间。\n\n```python\ndef wilson_ci(p, n, z=1.96):\n    denom = 1 + z**2 / n\n    center = (p + z**2 / (2*n)) / denom\n    half = z * np.sqrt((p*(1-p) + z**2/(4*n)) / n) / denom\n    return max(0, center - half), min(1, center + half)\n```\n\n### Pattern F：Per-label offset dict（解决标注重叠）\n\n**何时用**：scatter 上多个点需要标注且自动布局重叠。\n\n```python\nlabel_offsets = {\n    'Qwen3-8B':  (6, -12),   # 向下避让\n    'Qwen3-4B':  (-55, 4),   # 向左避让\n    'Llama-8B':  (6, 6),\n}\nfor name, (x, y) in data.items():\n    ox, oy = label_offsets.get(name, (6, 6))\n    ax.annotate(name, (x, y), xytext=(ox, oy), textcoords='offset points', fontsize=7)\n```\n\n### Pattern G：Hatching 加分类维度\n\n**何时用**：已用颜色表示一个维度，再加一个二元维度（如 collapse vs not）。\n\n```python\nfor is_collapse, bar in zip(collapse_flags, bars):\n    if is_collapse:\n        bar.set_hatch('///')\n        bar.set_alpha(0.7)\n```\n\n### Pattern H：Multi-panel 非对称布局\n\n**何时用**：主图 + 辅助 panel；或两个等重要 panel 但视觉重心需要倾斜。\n\n```python\nfig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(6.5, 3.2),\n                          gridspec_kw={'width_ratios': [1.4, 1]})\n# 主面板 1.4x 宽，副面板 1x；视觉重心在左\n```\n\n### Pattern I：Broken axis（异常值或对数 0）\n\n**何时用**：数据范围跨度大，或对数轴需要表示 0。\n\n```python\nd = 0.012\nkwargs = dict(transform=ax.transAxes, color='black', clip_on=False, linewidth=0.6)\nax.plot((1-d, 1+d), (-d, +d), **kwargs)  # 右下角斜线\nax.plot((1-d, 1+d), (1-d, 1+d), **kwargs)  # 右上角斜线\n```\n\n### Pattern J：Path-effect 文字描边\n\n**何时用**：标注必须放在数据点 / colored region 上方。\n\n```python\nimport matplotlib.patheffects as pe\nt = ax.text(x, y, 'label', fontsize=8, color='red')\nt.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])\n```\n\n### Pattern K：Concept curve（建立直觉）\n\n**何时用**：论文核心 claim 是定性现象，需要先让读者理解理论预期。\n\n```python\n# 完全合成的 cosine trajectory，不是实验数据\nx = np.linspace(0, 1, 100)\ntrajectory = baseline * (1 - np.cos(np.pi * x)) / 2\nax.plot(x, trajectory, linewidth=2, solid_capstyle='round')\nax.annotate('gap eliminated', (1.0, final_y), color='#388E3C',\n            fontstyle='italic', fontsize=9)\n```\n\n### Pattern L：Forest plot（meta-analysis 风格）\n\n**何时用**：多个子条件的 effect size + CI 对比，加 inline 统计数字。\n\n3-column GridSpec：(1) 标签 (2) CI 区间图 (3) 统计数字（mean, CI, p-value）\n\n每行：dot + horizontal line(CI) + 数字 inline。等价带用半透明 axvspan。\n\n### 概念图 L1 防丑原则（7 条）\n\n概念图（架构图、流程图、framework 示意）的视觉底线，与统计图 L1 互补。\n\n1. **Flow direction 一致** — 全图统一左→右或上→下，不混合方向\n2. **Box 规格统一** — 同层级 box 尺寸、圆角、边框粗细一致\n3. **箭头样式统一** — 不混用多种箭头类型（除非有语义区分）\n4. **层级清晰** — 不同抽象层用缩进/嵌套/背景色区分\n5. **密度控制** — 单张图 ≤15 个 box，超过则拆分或分组\n6. **Label 完整** — 每个 box 和箭头都有文字说明\n7. **无装饰性元素** — 不加纯装饰图标、渐变、3D 效果\n\n### 概念图 L2 叙事原则（6 条）\n\n让概念图不只是\"画了个框图\"，而是\"引导读者理解系统\"。\n\n1. **视觉锚点** — 核心贡献用饱和色/大字/加粗边框突出，其余 muted\n2. **阅读路径** — 沿箭头方向自然读完全图，不需要跳跃\n3. **对比布局** — 左右并列展示 before/after 或 baseline/ours\n4. **分组嵌套** — 用浅色背景框将相关组件分组（如 encoder/decoder）\n5. **箭头语义** — 实线=数据流，虚线=控制流/可选，粗线=关键路径\n6. **信息层级** — 主路径用饱和色+大字，辅助信息用灰色+小字\n\n### 概念图 L3 Pattern 库\n\n| Pattern | 名称 | 适用场景 |\n|---------|------|---------|\n| P-A | 线性流程 | 数据处理 pipeline、推理流程 |\n| P-B | 层级嵌套 | 模型架构（encoder/decoder/attention） |\n| P-C | 对比并列 | baseline vs ours、before/after |\n| P-D | 分支汇聚 | 多输入融合、ensemble |\n| P-E | 循环 | 训练循环、迭代优化 |\n| P-F | 诊断链 | 消融实验逻辑、排错流程 |\n| P-G | 三面板综合 | 概念+数据+结论（常用于 Figure 1） |\n\n### 概念图 Checklist（7 项）\n\n- [ ] Flow direction 全图统一（左→右或上→下）\n- [ ] Box 规格一致（同层级尺寸、圆角、边框相同）\n- [ ] 视觉层级清晰（核心突出，辅助 muted）\n- [ ] 核心贡献在视觉上最突出\n- [ ] 箭头语义一致（实线/虚线/粗细有意义）\n- [ ] 留白充足（box 之间不拥挤）\n- [ ] 跨图风格一致（配色、字号、线宽与论文其他图统一）\n\n---\n\n## L4 工具路由 + 模板\n\n### 路由规则\n\n| 场景 | 工具 | 原因 |\n|------|------|------|\n| 概念图（架构图、流程图、framework 示意） | **PaperBanana（Gemini）** | matplotlib 手写 boxes/arrows 极痛苦 |\n| 统计图（scatter、bar、line、heatmap、forest） | **matplotlib** | 确定性、可控、有模板 |\n| 离线 / 无 GOOGLE_API_KEY | **matplotlib** | 不依赖外部 API |\n\n项目级偏好：`config/projects/{name}.yaml` 的 `visualization.preferred_tool` 可覆盖。\n\n### Matplotlib 通用样式（rcParams 标准）\n\n```python\nimport matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport matplotlib.patheffects as pe\nimport numpy as np\n\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'DejaVu Sans',\n    'font.size': 11, 'axes.titlesize': 13, 'axes.labelsize': 12,\n    'xtick.labelsize': 10, 'ytick.labelsize': 10, 'legend.fontsize': 10,\n    'figure.dpi': 300, 'savefig.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.bbox': 'tight', 'savefig.pad_inches': 0.05,\n    'axes.spines.top': False, 'axes.spines.right': False,\n    'pdf.fonttype': 42, 'ps.fonttype': 42,  # TrueType 嵌入\n    'lines.linewidth': 1.8,\n})\n\n# 色盲友好语义色板（Okabe-Ito）\nSEMANTIC_COLORS = {\n    'baseline':   '#0072B2',  # blue - safe / mild\n    'treatment':  '#E69F00',  # orange - intervention\n    'collapse':   '#D55E00',  # vermillion - danger / catastrophic\n    'safe':       '#009E73',  # green - good outcome\n    'neutral':    '#999999',  # gray - reference / control\n}\n```\n\n### 常用模板（仅作起点，需按 narrative_intent 改造）\n\n详细执行模板见 `skills/global/draw-paper-figure.skill.md`：\n- Reliability Diagram（校准图）\n- ECE Heatmap（方法 × 数据集 ΔECE）\n- T* Scatter Plot（任务 Regime 可视化）\n\n**重要**：这些模板是起点不是终点。每张图根据 `narrative_intent` 必须加 L3 中的相关 pattern，不能直接套用。\n\n### 主图（Figure 1）特别提示\n\n大多数 ML 论文的 Figure 1 是概念图/架构图/方法概览——正是 PaperBanana 的强项。除非 Figure 1 是纯数值结果对比（如 Pareto frontier），否则优先用 PaperBanana。Figure 1 的 `narrative_intent` 必须与 `writing_plan.yaml` 的 `meta.core_claim` 直接关联。\n\n### PaperBanana 用法\n\n见 `skills/global/paper-banana.skill.md`。意图描述时直接引用 `narrative_intent`，并把 L3 中的 pattern 名写进 intent（如 \"with collapse zone background band, hollow markers for n<50\"）。\n\n---\n\n## 给 Paper Agent 的填写规则\n\n规划 `visualization_plan` 时，对每张图填：\n\n```yaml\n- fig_id: fig2_main_result\n  type: statistical\n  tool: matplotlib\n  data_source: \"registry: exp_001, exp_002\"\n  placement: experiments\n\n  # 新字段（必填）\n  narrative_intent: \"Quantized models match FP16 on accuracy but degrade calibration; ECE gap widens with bits→2.\"\n  design_notes:\n    - \"Pattern A: regime bands for bits=8/4/3\"\n    - \"Pattern C: dashed line for FP16 ECE baseline\"\n    - \"Pattern D: hollow markers for n<50 data points\"\n\n  aspect_ratio: \"4:3\"\n```\n\n`narrative_intent`：一句话，主动语态，描述读者应该相信的事。\n\n`design_notes`：从 L3 选 0-4 个 pattern，给出选用理由。可以为空（说明默认模板足够）。\n\n---\n\n## 提交前 Checklist（以下为统计图专用）\n\nL1 部分（防丑底线）：\n- [ ] 所有轴有 label + 单位\n- [ ] 无 dual y-axis、无 3D、无 pie\n- [ ] 颜色绑定语义、色盲友好\n- [ ] Bar chart 从 0 起（如有）\n- [ ] 字号 ≥ 10pt（缩到 column width 后仍可读）\n- [ ] PDF 矢量、300 DPI\n\nL2 部分（叙事强度）：\n- [ ] 能用一句话写出 figure takeaway（应等于 `narrative_intent`）\n- [ ] Subtitle / panel label 是 insight，不是描述\n- [ ] 数据质量差异在视觉上可见（n、OOS、pending 等）\n- [ ] 至少考虑过是否需要 background band / fill_between / reference line（不一定要加）\n\nL3 部分（高级感——不可跳过）：\n- [ ] 不像 matplotlib gallery 示例（无默认灰背景、无粗网格、无 tab10 色板）\n- [ ] 配色 muted 和谐，不刺眼，不过度饱和\n- [ ] 线宽、字号、间距全图统一\n- [ ] 元素对齐严格（bars 底部对齐、labels 水平对齐）\n- [ ] 留白充足（元素之间不拥挤）\n- [ ] 与论文全文其他图的风格一致\n- [ ] 终极判断：这张图放在 NeurIPS best paper 里违和吗？\n",

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01CKcj2oa88FfBHrvzZrj8ft`</summary>

````text
1	---
2	description: 用 PaperBanana 生成高品质学术概念图（架构/流程/framework）。统计图不要用 PaperBanana，用 draw-paper-figure。需要 GOOGLE_API_KEY。
3	name: paper-banana
4	scope: global
5	tags:
6	  - visualization
7	  - paper
8	  - matplotlib
9	  - llm-codegen
10	  - conceptual-diagram
11	version: 4
12	tools:
13	  - request_secret
14	  - poll_secret_request
15	---
16	
17	# Paper Banana — 高品质学术概念图生成
18	
19	PaperBanana 由 Gemini Pro 自动写 matplotlib 代码，VLM Critic 评估渲染结果并迭代优化，输出顶会级别 300 DPI 概念图。
20	
21	**质量底线：每张图必须达到顶会论文的美观度——高级感、专业感、精致感。绝不接受 matplotlib 默认样式的廉价感。**
22	
23	## 适用范围
24	
25	| 场景 | 用什么 |
26	|------|--------|
27	| **概念图**（架构图、流程图、framework 示意） | **PaperBanana** ← 本 skill |
28	| **统计图**（scatter、bar、line、heatmap、forest） | **draw-paper-figure.skill.md**。**不要用 PaperBanana 画统计图** |
29	| 离线 / 无 GOOGLE_API_KEY | **draw-paper-figure.skill.md** |
30	
31	## 执行环境
32	
33	**PaperBanana 在本机（EC2）运行，不要 SSH 到远程服务器。**
34	
35	EC2（ap-northeast-1 东京）可直接访问 Gemini API，无需代理。
36	
37	## 前置条件
38	
39	### Step 1：获取 GOOGLE_API_KEY
40	
41	```python
42	request_secret(key_id="google_api_key", project="&lt;project_name>", purpose="PaperBanana 概念图生成")
43	# 等待 Director 审批后
44	poll_secret_request(request_id="<返回的 request_id>", project="&lt;project_name>")
45	```
46	
47	如果密钥尚未存储，告知 Director 需要 GOOGLE_API_KEY。
48	
49	### Step 2：安装验证（每次使用前必须检查）
50	
51	```bash
52	# 检查是否已安装
53	paperbanana --version 2>/dev/null || {
54	  echo "PaperBanana 未安装，正在安装..."
55	  pip install paperbanana -q
56	  paperbanana --version
57	}
58	```
59	
60	如果 `paperbanana --version` 失败（命令不存在），必须先 `pip install paperbanana -q` 安装。版本需 ≥ 0.1.2。
61	
62	**设计前必读** `skills/global/paper-visualization.skill.md` 的概念图 L1（防丑 7 条）+ L2（叙事 6 条）+ L3 Pattern + Checklist。统计图部分不适用于 PaperBanana。
63	
64	## 命令：generate
65	
66	概念图用 `generate` 子命令。**必须显式指定 `--vlm-model gemini-2.5-pro`**（默认模型 `gemini-2.0-flash` 已废弃，不指定会崩溃）。**`--iterations 5`**。
67	
68	## 完整流程
69	
70	### Step 1：写意图描述文件
71	
72	将详细设计描述写入文件（太长不适合命令行参数）：
73	
74	```bash
75	cat > /tmp/fig_intent.txt << 'INTENT'
76	Three-panel academic figure for a top-venue paper (full width, aspect ratio ~3.2:1, figsize 14x4.5 inches):
77	
78	Panel (a) 'The Phenomenon' (left, ~38% width):
79	Grouped bar chart. TF bars: dark navy (#2C3E50). AR bars: academic red (#C0392B).
80	Exact data: Base TF=0.501 AR=0.019, ThreadGate TF=0.504 AR=0.064, ...
81	Y-axis 'Mid-turn GAR' range 0–0.55. Only left+bottom spines. Serif font 10pt.
82	Add bracket annotation '8-17x gap' between bar groups.
83	
84	Panel (b) 'Diagnostic Chain' (center, ~24% width):
85	Vertical flowchart, 4 rounded boxes connected by thin gray arrows:
86	  Step 1: light gray (#F5F6F7) box, thin border (#DEE2E6), 'Gate Analysis' + subtitle. Green ✓
87	  Step 2: same style, 'PCGrad Exclusion'. Red ✗
88	  Step 3: same style, 'Representation Check'. Red ✗
89	  Step 4: light green (#D4EDDA) background, 'Conclusion: Exposure bias'. Green ✓
90	No axes, no spines. Clean infographic style.
91	
92	Panel (c) 'Constructive Evidence' (right, ~38% width):
93	Line plot. Data points: (0, 0.064), (3, 0.556), (5, 0.727).
94	Navy line (#2C3E50), open circles (white fill, navy edge, width 2).
95	Light blue fill (#EBF5FB, alpha 0.4) under curve.
96	Annotate '11x recovery' in muted gray, 9pt italic.
97	
98	OVERALL STYLE:
99	- 300 DPI, white background, publication quality
100	- Serif font (Times New Roman or similar) throughout
101	- Panel labels '(a)', '(b)', '(c)' in 11pt bold at top-left
102	- Generous white space (wspace=0.35)
103	- Muted academic palette: navy #2C3E50, red #C0392B, green #27AE60, gray #7F8C8D
104	- NO matplotlib default styling: no gray background, no thick gridlines, no tab10 colors
105	- Drop shadows on boxes: subtle, 2px offset, 10% opacity
106	- Rounded corners 8px on all rectangles
107	- Arrow heads: small, elegant, not bulky
108	INTENT
109	```
110	
111	### Step 2：运行 generate
112	
113	```bash
114	GOOGLE_API_KEY="[REDACTED]" paperbanana generate \
115	  --input /tmp/fig_intent.txt \
116	  --caption "Three-panel diagnostic framework: (a) TF vs AR performance gap, (b) systematic diagnosis chain, (c) beta-steering recovery evidence" \
117	  --vlm-model gemini-2.5-pro \
118	  --output figures/paper/fig1_overview.png \
119	  --iterations 5
120	```
121	
122	### Step 3：视觉检查（不可跳过）
123	
124	**Read 生成的 PNG 文件**，视觉评估以下维度：
125	
126	1. **高级感**：是否有顶会论文的精致感？还是看起来像课程作业？
127	2. **颜色**：是否有语义（不是随机分配）？是否和谐、不刺眼？
128	3. **文字**：缩到 column width 后是否 ≥ 7pt 可读？
129	4. **布局**：留白是否合理？元素是否对齐？
130	5. **细节**：圆角、阴影、箭头是否精致？边框是否统一？
131	
132	**不满意 → 必须修改意图描述文件，重新生成。**
133	
134	### Step 4：转 PDF
135	
136	```bash
137	python3 -c "
138	import matplotlib; matplotlib.use('Agg')
139	import matplotlib.pyplot as plt, matplotlib.image as mpimg
140	img = mpimg.imread('figures/paper/fig1_overview.png')
141	h, w = img.shape[:2]
142	fig = plt.figure(figsize=(w/300, h/300), dpi=300)
143	ax = fig.add_axes([0,0,1,1]); ax.imshow(img); ax.axis('off')
144	plt.savefig('figures/paper/fig1_overview.pdf', bbox_inches='tight', pad_inches=0)
145	plt.close()
146	"
147	```
148	
149	## 意图描述质量红线
150	
151	Intent 质量直接决定图的质量。必须包含以下 7 项（缺一不可）：
152	
153	1. **空间布局**：每个 panel 的宽度占比（如 38%/24%/38%）
154	2. **组件形状 + 尺寸**：圆角半径 8px、figsize (14, 4.5)
155	3. **颜色 hex 码**：不说"蓝色"，说 "#2C3E50"，绑定语义
156	4. **所有文字内容**：box label、副标题、箭头 annotation
157	5. **尺寸规格**：DPI 300、字号 10-12pt body / 8-9pt labels
158	6. **细节规格**：阴影偏移 2px / 10% opacity、箭头粗细、边框宽度
159	7. **反面约束**："NO matplotlib default styling, NO gray background, NO tab10 colors"
160	
161	### 美学风格关键词（必须在 intent 中出现）
162	
163	```
164	STYLE REQUIREMENTS:
165	- Publication quality for a TOP venue (NeurIPS/ICML/EMNLP)
166	- Elegant, premium, sophisticated — NOT cheap or homework-like
167	- Subtle drop shadows (2px, 10% opacity) on boxes
168	- Rounded corners (8px) on all rectangles
169	- Thin, elegant arrow heads — not bulky matplotlib defaults
170	- Serif font (Times) for labels, sans-serif (Helvetica) for annotations
171	- Muted, harmonious color palette — NO saturated primary colors
172	- Generous white space between elements
173	- Consistent visual weight across all panels
174	```
175	
176	### 反面教材（这样的 intent 一定画不好）
177	
178	- "Draw a pipeline diagram with three stages"
179	- "Architecture diagram for our system"
180	
181	太模糊。LLM 会用 matplotlib 默认样式填充所有你没指定的细节，结果就是廉价感。
182	
183	## 迭代策略（至少 3 轮外部迭代，Hero Figure 至少 5 轮）
184	
185	PaperBanana 内部有 VLM Critic 迭代（`--iterations 5`），但这还不够。你必须做**外部迭代**：
186	
187	### 第 1 轮：基础生成
188	运行 PaperBanana → Read PNG → 识别大方向问题（布局、配色、结构）
189	
190	### 第 2 轮：结构修复
191	修改意图描述（布局占比、颜色 hex、组件关系）→ 重新运行 → Read PNG
192	
193	### 第 3 轮：细节打磨
194	修改意图描述（字号、阴影、对齐、箭头粗细）→ 重新运行 → Read PNG
195	
196	### 第 4 轮（Hero Figure）：风格统一
197	线宽 / 字号 / 间距全图统一？与论文其他图配色一致？→ 重新运行 → Read PNG
198	
199	### 第 5 轮（Hero Figure）：最终 polish
200	像素级检查 → 缩到 column width 后文字 ≥ 7pt？→ 终极判断：放在 NeurIPS best paper 里违和吗？
201	
202	### 判断标准
203	
204	**可以交付**：看起来像 NeurIPS 2025 best paper 里的图。
205	**不可交付**：看起来像 matplotlib gallery 的示例图。
206	
207	## 失败回退
208	
209	如果 PaperBanana 多次崩溃（API 错误、模型不兼容）：
210	
211	1. 确认 `--vlm-model gemini-2.5-pro` 是否指定
212	2. 确认 GOOGLE_API_KEY 是否有效：`curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models?key=$GOOGLE_API_KEY"` 应返回 200
213	3. 如果仍然失败，回退 matplotlib 手写：
214	   - Read `skills/global/paper-visualization.skill.md` 获取 L1-L4 设计标准
215	   - 写 Python 脚本手动画概念图（FancyBboxPatch、FancyArrowPatch 等）
216	   - **同样必须 Read PNG 做视觉检查，至少迭代 3 轮**
217	   - **同样必须达到顶会级别的美观度**
218	   - 保存 .py 脚本到 figures/paper/ 目录便于后续修改
219	
````

systematic diagnosis chain, (c) beta-steering recovery evidence\" \\\n  --vlm-model gemini-2.5-pro \\\n  --output figures/paper/fig1_overview.png \\\n  --iterations 5\n```\n\n### Step 3：视觉检查（不可跳过）\n\n**Read 生成的 PNG 文件**，视觉评估以下维度：\n\n1. **高级感**：是否有顶会论文的精致感？还是看起来像课程作业？\n2. **颜色**：是否有语义（不是随机分配）？是否和谐、不刺眼？\n3. **文字**：缩到 column width 后是否 ≥ 7pt 可读？\n4. **布局**：留白是否合理？元素是否对齐？\n5. **细节**：圆角、阴影、箭头是否精致？边框是否统一？\n\n**不满意 → 必须修改意图描述文件，重新生成。**\n\n### Step 4：转 PDF\n\n```bash\npython3 -c \"\nimport matplotlib; matplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt, matplotlib.image as mpimg\nimg = mpimg.imread('figures/paper/fig1_overview.png')\nh, w = img.shape[:2]\nfig = plt.figure(figsize=(w/300, h/300), dpi=300)\nax = fig.add_axes([0,0,1,1]); ax.imshow(img); ax.axis('off')\nplt.savefig('figures/paper/fig1_overview.pdf', bbox_inches='tight', pad_inches=0)\nplt.close()\n\"\n```\n\n## 意图描述质量红线\n\nIntent 质量直接决定图的质量。必须包含以下 7 项（缺一不可）：\n\n1. **空间布局**：每个 panel 的宽度占比（如 38%/24%/38%）\n2. **组件形状 + 尺寸**：圆角半径 8px、figsize (14, 4.5)\n3. **颜色 hex 码**：不说\"蓝色\"，说 \"#2C3E50\"，绑定语义\n4. **所有文字内容**：box label、副标题、箭头 annotation\n5. **尺寸规格**：DPI 300、字号 10-12pt body / 8-9pt labels\n6. **细节规格**：阴影偏移 2px / 10% opacity、箭头粗细、边框宽度\n7. **反面约束**：\"NO matplotlib default styling, NO gray background, NO tab10 colors\"\n\n### 美学风格关键词（必须在 intent 中出现）\n\n```\nSTYLE REQUIREMENTS:\n- Publication quality for a TOP venue (NeurIPS/ICML/EMNLP)\n- Elegant, premium, sophisticated — NOT cheap or homework-like\n- Subtle drop shadows (2px, 10% opacity) on boxes\n- Rounded corners (8px) on all rectangles\n- Thin, elegant arrow heads — not bulky matplotlib defaults\n- Serif font (Times) for labels, sans-serif (Helvetica) for annotations\n- Muted, harmonious color palette — NO saturated primary colors\n- Generous white space between elements\n- Consistent visual weight across all panels\n```\n\n### 反面教材（这样的 intent 一定画不好）\n\n- \"Draw a pipeline diagram with three stages\"\n- \"Architecture diagram for our system\"\n\n太模糊。LLM 会用 matplotlib 默认样式填充所有你没指定的细节，结果就是廉价感。\n\n## 迭代策略（至少 3 轮外部迭代，Hero Figure 至少 5 轮）\n\nPaperBanana 内部有 VLM Critic 迭代（`--iterations 5`），但这还不够。你必须做**外部迭代**：\n\n### 第 1 轮：基础生成\n运行 PaperBanana → Read PNG → 识别大方向问题（布局、配色、结构）\n\n### 第 2 轮：结构修复\n修改意图描述（布局占比、颜色 hex、组件关系）→ 重新运行 → Read PNG\n\n### 第 3 轮：细节打磨\n修改意图描述（字号、阴影、对齐、箭头粗细）→ 重新运行 → Read PNG\n\n### 第 4 轮（Hero Figure）：风格统一\n线宽 / 字号 / 间距全图统一？与论文其他图配色一致？→ 重新运行 → Read PNG\n\n### 第 5 轮（Hero Figure）：最终 polish\n像素级检查 → 缩到 column width 后文字 ≥ 7pt？→ 终极判断：放在 NeurIPS best paper 里违和吗？\n\n### 判断标准\n\n**可以交付**：看起来像 NeurIPS 2025 best paper 里的图。\n**不可交付**：看起来像 matplotlib gallery 的示例图。\n\n## 失败回退\n\n如果 PaperBanana 多次崩溃（API 错误、模型不兼容）：\n\n1. 确认 `--vlm-model gemini-2.5-pro` 是否指定\n2. 确认 GOOGLE_API_KEY 是否有效：`curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' \"https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models?key=$GOOGLE_API_KEY\"` 应返回 200\n3. 如果仍然失败，回退 matplotlib 手写：\n   - Read `skills/global/paper-visualization.skill.md` 获取 L1-L4 设计标准\n   - 写 Python 脚本手动画概念图（FancyBboxPatch、FancyArrowPatch 等）\n   - **同样必须 Read PNG 做视觉检查，至少迭代 3 轮**\n   - **同样必须达到顶会级别的美观度**\n   - 保存 .py 脚本到 figures/paper/ 目录便于后续修改\n",

Let me analyze the current figure visually:

**Visual Diagnosis of Figure 2 (label_decomposition.pdf):**

1. **Overall impression**: This is a stacked/segmented horizontal bar chart showing episode-level label decomposition. It's a matplotlib-generated statistical chart.

2. **Problems identified**:
   - **Matplotlib default feel**: The colors (gray, red, orange, blue) are quite saturated and feel like default tab10-ish colors
   - **The bracket annotation** at the top ("75/95 non-tie = 78.9%") is rendered with matplotlib's default bracket style - looks a bit crude
   - **Legend styling** has an outdated border frame, and the color palette doesn't match the project specs (should use #2563EB for baseline, #DC2626 for treatment, #6B7280 for collapse, #D1D5DB for safe, #2166AC for neutral)
   - **Visual balance issues**: The gray background dominates despite being the least interesting segment, the numbers inside bars are oversized and bold, and all segments carry equal weight when some deserve more emphasis
   - **Typography**: Using default sans-serif that lacks refinement

The core narrative here is that death asymmetry drives 78.9% of the non-tie routing signal across 245 episodes—that's the dominant story to tell. The user wants this converted to PaperBanana style and cleaned up to eliminate text overlap, so I need to redesign this as a beautiful visualization using that framework while respecting the user's explicit tool choice, even though statistical charts typically avoid that approach.

I'll start by retrieving the API key, verifying PaperBanana is available, then crafting a detailed intent file that captures the narrative before running the visualization.

Display \"150\" centered in segment, font 14pt, color #6B7280.\n  Segment 2 (next, 54/245 width): \"Death-only signal\" — color #DC2626 (academic red), Display \"54\" centered, font 13pt, white text.\n  Segment 3 (next, 21/245 width): \"Death + score\" — color #E8825A (muted coral/orange, harmonizes with red), Display \"21\" centered, font 12pt, white text.\n  Segment 4 (rightmost, 20/245 width): \"Non-death score\" — color #2563EB (academic blue), Display \"20\" centered, font 12pt, white text.\n\n  Thin 1px white gaps between segments for visual separation.\n\nANNOTATIONS ABOVE THE BAR:\n  A thin elegant bracket (not thick matplotlib default) spanning segments 2+3 (the two death-asymmetry segments), with a label above:\n    \"75 / 95 non-tie = 78.9%\"\n    Font: 11pt, bold, color #374151 (dark gray)\n    Subtitle below the number: \"death-asymmetry labels\"\n    Font: 9pt, italic, color #6B7280\n\n  Another thin bracket spanning segments 2+3+4 (all non-tie), with a smaller label:\n    \"95 non-tie episodes\"\n    Font: 9pt, color #9CA3AF (medium gray)\n\nANNOTATIONS BELOW THE BAR:\n  Category labels below each segment, vertically aligned, 9pt font, color #4B5563:\n    Below segment 1: \"No preference\" (centered under segment)\n    Below segment 2: \"Death-asym.:\" line break \"sole signal\" (centered under segment)\n    Below segment 3: \"Death-asym.:\" line break \"death + score\" (centered under segment)\n    Below segment 4: \"Non-death\" line break \"score diff.\" (centered under segment)\n\n  Small colored dots (●) before each label matching segment color for redundant encoding.\n\n  Below all labels, a subtle x-axis line with label: \"Episodes (N = 245)\" in 10pt, color #6B7280.\n\nVISUAL HIERARCHY:\n  - Death-asymmetry segments (red + coral) are the visual focus — saturated, prominent\n  - \"No preference\" segment is deliberately muted (#D1D5DB) — it's context, not the finding\n  - Non-death segment (blue) is medium prominence — it's the contrast category\n  - The \"78.9%\" annotation is the takeaway — largest annotation font, bold\n\nOVERALL STYLE:\n  - 300 DPI, pure white background (#FFFFFF), publication quality\n  - Serif font (Times New Roman or similar) for numbers and key statistics\n  - Sans-serif (Helvetica/Arial) for category labels and small annotations\n  - NO matplotlib default styling: NO gray background, NO gridlines, NO tick marks, NO axes frame, NO spines\n  - NO legend box — labels are directly below segments\n  - Subtle drop shadow on the bar segments (1px offset, 5% opacity, soft blur)\n  - Rounded corners (4px) on the leftmost and rightmost bar ends only\n  - Generous white space: minimum 0.5 inch margin on all sides\n  - Clean, infographic aesthetic — think data journalism, not matplotlib gallery\n  - All text must be non-overlapping with generous spacing\n  - Bracket annotations use thin lines (0.8pt) with small serif ends, not thick matplotlib ConnectionPatch\n  - Color palette: muted academic — gray #D1D5DB, red #DC2626, coral #E8825A, blue #2563EB, text #374151/#6B7280\n",
